Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in meteorology theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 984 and the invariant I(A,B) = 89 mod 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the meteorology space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 984 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 89. QED.
